# Manifest of the Quake Live .pk3 archives - FILE NAMES ONLY.
#
# No asset content here: this is a directory listing, not the pak, so it does not
# fall under the rule that pak00.pk3 is never committed or shipped. It is checked
# in so asset names can be verified instead of guessed - RE_RegisterShader and
# RE_RegisterModel both return 0 for a name the pak does not contain, which
# renders nothing and reports nothing.
#
# Regenerate after a game patch with tools/dump-pak-manifest.ps1 (see CLAUDE.md).
# Format: <pak file><TAB><path inside it>
#
# Note for grepping: 'ice' also matches qzpractICE1, slICE_12, vOICE_window and
# offICE_door. Read the hits, do not count them.
#
